#-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# [dt64-range] client: DateTime64 outside 1677-09-21 .. 2262-04-11 is wrong when read and when written
#   The nanosecond value overflows int64: ch-go proto.DateTime64.Time when
#   reading, clickhouse-go DateTime64.timeToInt64 (t.UnixNano) when writing.
#   No error.
#   Reproduction:
#     SELECT toDateTime64('1600-01-01 00:00:00', 3, 'UTC')  -- driver:
#     2184-07-20 23:34:33.709
